Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on microbiology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 334. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 576 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by microbiology generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 576. QED.
